# Media attribution and reuse

Images are extracted regions of original published figures or a labeled text excerpt. Source content was not redrawn. The ACL key-frame GIFs select actual video frames and adds a separate timestamp strip; the Flick-in GIF is copied unchanged. Original authorship is retained. The atlas annotations describe source evidence and are separate from the underlying media.

ACL materials before 2016 use CC BY-NC-SA 3.0; subsequent ACL materials use CC BY 4.0. This policy does not automatically cover third-party publications hosted by the Anthology. [ACL copyright policy](https://aclanthology.org/faq/copyright/). The reused ACL items here are ACL proceedings/workshop material. Non-ACL figure licenses are stated in the original PDFs.

## tang2015touch-6m5KuF5MIbY

## wada2025flick-video

Wada, Yuto and Choi, Myungguen and Shirane, Kaoru and Shizuki, Buntarou. 2025. *Flick-in: Japanese Text Entry Method for Indirect Touch Using Bezel-Initiated Swipe*.

[Original source](https://github.com/inaniwaudon/flick-in). External source only; no local reproduction.

Author repository links the official supplementary video and an animated demonstration. Evaluated output is kana; Kanji conversion was not implemented.

## wada2025flick-animation

## jia2014joint-keyframes

## Original paper excerpts

These source excerpts were selected from the user-provided article PDFs for the review companion. Rights remain with the original rights holders; this section does not assign an open license. Figures, tables and page previews are identified separately. Figure coordinates, source hashes and visual checks are in `figure-selections.json`.



- **ezaki2000pen** — [A pen-based Japanese character input system for the blind person](https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/abstract/document/902936/). Ezaki, N. and Hikichi, T. and Kiyota, K. and Yamamoto, S. (2000). figure 5, included article PDF page 3. File: `media/paper-figures/ezaki2000pen.png`.

- **luk2000digital** — [Digital library access for chinese visually impaired](https://dl.acm.org/doi/abs/10.1145/336597.336678). Luk, Robert and Yeung, Daniel and Lu, Qin and Leung, Eric and Li, S. Y. and Leung, Fred (2000). figure 2, included article PDF page 2. File: `media/paper-figures/luk2000digital.png`.

- **lin2006design** — [Design and implementation of a chorded on-screen keyboard for people with physical impairments](https://link.springer.com/chapter/10.1007/11788713_143). Lin, Yun-Lung and Chen, Ming-Chung and Yeh, Yao-Ming and Tzeng, Wen-Jeng and Yeh, Chih-Ching (2006). figure 1, included article PDF page 3. File: `media/paper-figures/lin2006design.png`.

- **lin2008designing** — [Designing a scanning on-screen keyboard for people with severe motor disabilities](https://link.springer.com/chapter/10.1007/978-3-540-70540-6_178). Lin, Yun-Lung and Wu, Ting-Fang and Chen, Ming-Chung and Yeh, Yao-Ming and Wang, Hwa-Pey (2008). figure 2, included article PDF page 3. File: `media/paper-figures/lin2008designing.png`.

- **fu2009fast** — [A fast text-based communication system for handicapped aphasiacs](https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/abstract/document/5337389/). Fu, Yu-Fen and Ho, Cheng-Seen (2009). figure 6, included article PDF page 12. File: `media/paper-figures/fu2009fast.png`.

- **kotani2010japanese** — [R&D of the Japanese Input Method using an eye-controlled communication device for users with disabilities and evaluation with NIRS](https://doi.org/10.1109/icsmc.2010.5641921). Kotani, Shinji and Watanabe, Hiromi and Komasaki, Tetsu and Ohgi, Kazuhiro and Yamamoto, Yoshihiko (2010). figure 10, included article PDF page 3. File: `media/paper-figures/kotani2010japanese.png`.

- **minett2010assistive** — [An assistive communication brain-computer interface for Chinese text input](https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/abstract/document/5515559/). ## Additional conference videos and key frames

Added 2026-09-09. Video GIFs preserve original video frames and include a separate timestamp strip. Paper-figure slideshows retain original figure order. Paper-excerpt slideshows start with a reviewed illustration, table or preview, followed by source pages. Full-page views preserve multipart figures when separate crop boundaries are uncertain. All slideshows retain source links; their timing is editorial. External-only records do not assign a reuse license to the videos.

### sun2024exploring-video

Sun, Bin and Li, Jianfeng and Zhou, Hao and Meng, Fandong and Li, Kan and Zhou, Jie (2024). *Exploring Conditional Variational Mechanism to Pinyin Input Method for Addressing One-to-Many Mappings in Low-Resource Scenarios*.

[Source](https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-short.56/). [Original video](https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-short.56.mp4).

Conference-talk key frames: nine-/twenty-six-key Pinyin input, “bei zi” mapping to 被子 / 杯子 / 辈子, and the conditional variational model. These are explanatory slides, not a recorded typing trial.

External source only; no local reproduction.
